But these are live-green cases, not proof of full app parity for all variants.

Known gaps to handle in plan/execution:
- Need a central capability id/name per product behavior. Current concepts are messy: task_type, route_key, template_id, workflow id, live case name, and actual product capability are mixed.
- Need capability contract registry/report generator tying app capabilities -> task types -> route keys -> Wan2GP behavior/defaults -> VibeComfy implementation/template/scratchpad -> variant axes -> output artifact contract -> static/live validation evidence.
- Need variant-aware coverage: LoRAs, multiple LoRAs, URL LoRAs, phase-scheduled LoRAs, control methods raw/flow/canny/depth/pose/mask, source media variants, resolution/frame/FPS/profile/seed/prompt/negative prompt/interpolation/postprocess/output.
- Need not brute-force all combinations; use risk-based pairwise/interaction coverage. Every app-exposed axis value needs at least one proof or fail-closed rule; known interactions need explicit cases.
- Need distinguish non-Comfy paths like orchestration/stitching: these may remain WGP/non-template but still need contract validation for child creation, route metadata, DB state, and artifact behavior.
- Need use VibeComfy validators rather than reimplementing graph/schema/custom node/model validation in worker.
- Need tooling that helps agents/developers import, fork, or build workflows from scratch: draft contracts, inspect/explain, validate, diff, next-step guidance, status labels.

Prep phase instructions:
1. Inventory current tooling in reigh-worker, VibeComfy, and relevant reigh-app/Supabase enqueue surfaces.
2. Determine where app capabilities are defined/enqueued, without changing reigh-app unless explicitly necessary.
3. Inspect existing live matrix/report parsing affordances and any docs/tests around migration-baselines, worker matrix, template routing, artifact contracts, VibeComfy preflight.
4. Inspect VibeComfy CLI/docs/tests enough to avoid duplicating its responsibilities.
5. Identify the smallest executable first slice that creates real leverage, not just prose.

Desired high-level architecture:
- Capability = product behavior users care about.
- Route = execution decision for a capability variant.
- Implementation = WGP or VibeComfy binding.
- Template = VibeComfy implementation detail.
- Workflow = source/import artifact.
- Live case = validation sample/evidence.

Expected implementation direction:
- Add a machine-readable capability contract registry in reigh-worker, likely under scripts/capability_contracts/ or source/task_handlers/tasks/ depending on repo patterns.
- Add a CLI/report generator, e.g. python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report or similar, with commands/status output that can merge: registry, template_routing.py, scripts/live_test/matrix.py, ../vibecomfy/template_index.json, ../vibecomfy/workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json, and latest scripts/live_test/runs/*/report.md.
- Generate or update docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md from data rather than hand-maintaining it.
- Add contract tests that fail if a vibecomfy_supported route lacks a contract row, VibeComfy template reference, output artifact contract, variant accounting, or live evidence pointer.
- Add explicit status vocabulary: unknown/inventoried/routed/template_bound/static_valid/runtime_green/parity_reviewed/app_ready, plus wgp_only_contract_validated and unsupported_fail_closed where appropriate.
- Add next-action/status output so a developer/agent can see what blocks 100% coverage.
- Keep implementation bounded: first slice can be registry + report + tests + generated tracker + using it to identify and close obvious holes. Then continue improving if needed.

Success criteria:
MUST:
- Use megaplan standard workflow with prep -> plan -> critique -> gate -> finalize -> execute -> review.
- Build code, not only docs, unless prep proves there is already an equivalent working tool.
- Do not duplicate VibeComfy workflow validators in reigh-worker; call/reference/consume VibeComfy outputs instead.
- Produce a durable, checked-in capability-contract source of truth and generated/reportable parity status.
- Cover every current live matrix VibeComfy-supported route with a contract row or an explicit justified exception.
- Add tests proving route/template/live-evidence coverage cannot silently drift.
- Keep dirty unrelated app work intact.
SHOULD:
- Include app capability source references where practical.
- Include variant axes for LoRA/control/source media/resolution/frame/FPS/profile/postprocess/output, even if some entries are initially gap/fail-closed.
- Make the developer/agent UX discoverable through commands/docs.
- Use the new tooling after building it to update the tracker and identify next parity gaps.
INFO:
- Future directions include import/fork/new workflow workbench UX, but the first implementation should land the central capability contract and report loop.

        Idea:
Build and use a robust capability-contract and workflow-workbench toolchain that gets Reigh to 100% app parity coverage for VibeComfy vs existing Wan2GP/app behavior, while leaving reusable tools for future internal/community workflows, workflow forks, and scratch-built workflows.

Current situation and context:
- Repos live under /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace.
- Main implementation repo for this run is reigh-worker. Current durable branch should be megaplan/vibecomfy-sprint-09-control-rail-travel-matrix.
- VibeComfy repo is ../vibecomfy on branch megaplan/production-parity-templates.
- reigh-app has a dirty working tree on its own branch; inspect it as source of app capability truth but do not casually commit or revert its changes.
- The user wants actual execution after planning, not just a design doc. Keep going until the new tooling is built, then use it to continue closing validation/parity gaps.
- The user explicitly requested this run in a GPT-5.5 medium subagent. This current agent is that subagent. The local megaplan CLI supports profiles and phase model overrides, but no installed profile exposes an explicit GPT-5.5 medium model string. Use the all-codex profile / Codex-compatible routing where possible and note that limitation.

Important current facts:
- reigh-worker has a new tracker doc at docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md.
- reigh-worker has route contracts in source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py.
- reigh-worker has live matrix in scripts/live_test/matrix.py and live reports in scripts/live_test/runs/*/report.md.
- reigh-worker has VibeComfy adapter/scratchpads in source/models/comfy/vibecomfy_adapter.py.
- reigh-worker has worker preflight checks for VibeComfy template_index.json and workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json in source/runtime/worker/preflight.py.
- reigh-worker has health route labels in source/runtime/worker/health_labels.py.
- VibeComfy already owns workflow/template mechanics. Do not rebuild these in reigh-worker: python -m vibecomfy.cli port check, port convert, port widgets, validate, doctor, nodes install-plan/install/restore, fetch, workflows list, inspect, run, runpod; template_index.json; workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json; ready_templates; model registry/staging; custom-node pack registry; RunPod/corpus machinery.
- A subagent history audit found oldest local VibeComfy commit around 2026-04-26, not March. It concluded: VibeComfy should own Comfy implementation truth; reigh-worker should own app/product task truth, routing, Wan2GP parity, DB/storage artifact semantics, and live app validation.

Validated live-green VibeComfy-supported cases from current tracker include:
- individual_travel_segment_wan22_vace raw/flow/canny/depth
- travel_segment_wan22_vace raw/flow/canny/depth video_source
- join_clips_segment_wan22_vace
- qwen_image_style
- qwen_image_t2i
- qwen_image_2512
- wan_2_2_t2i
- wan_2_2_i2v
- animate_character
- image_upscale
- video_enhance
- flux_klein_edit
- qwen_image_edit
- image_inpaint
- annotated_image_edit
- z_image_turbo
- z_image_turbo_i2i
But these are live-green cases, not proof of full app parity for all variants.

Known gaps to handle in plan/execution:
- Need a central capability id/name per product behavior. Current concepts are messy: task_type, route_key, template_id, workflow id, live case name, and actual product capability are mixed.
- Need capability contract registry/report generator tying app capabilities -> task types -> route keys -> Wan2GP behavior/defaults -> VibeComfy implementation/template/scratchpad -> variant axes -> output artifact contract -> static/live validation evidence.
- Need variant-aware coverage: LoRAs, multiple LoRAs, URL LoRAs, phase-scheduled LoRAs, control methods raw/flow/canny/depth/pose/mask, source media variants, resolution/frame/FPS/profile/seed/prompt/negative prompt/interpolation/postprocess/output.
- Need not brute-force all combinations; use risk-based pairwise/interaction coverage. Every app-exposed axis value needs at least one proof or fail-closed rule; known interactions need explicit cases.
- Need distinguish non-Comfy paths like orchestration/stitching: these may remain WGP/non-template but still need contract validation for child creation, route metadata, DB state, and artifact behavior.
- Need use VibeComfy validators rather than reimplementing graph/schema/custom node/model validation in worker.
- Need tooling that helps agents/developers import, fork, or build workflows from scratch: draft contracts, inspect/explain, validate, diff, next-step guidance, status labels.

Prep phase instructions:
1. Inventory current tooling in reigh-worker, VibeComfy, and relevant reigh-app/Supabase enqueue surfaces.
2. Determine where app capabilities are defined/enqueued, without changing reigh-app unless explicitly necessary.
3. Inspect existing live matrix/report parsing affordances and any docs/tests around migration-baselines, worker matrix, template routing, artifact contracts, VibeComfy preflight.
4. Inspect VibeComfy CLI/docs/tests enough to avoid duplicating its responsibilities.
5. Identify the smallest executable first slice that creates real leverage, not just prose.

Desired high-level architecture:
- Capability = product behavior users care about.
- Route = execution decision for a capability variant.
- Implementation = WGP or VibeComfy binding.
- Template = VibeComfy implementation detail.
- Workflow = source/import artifact.
- Live case = validation sample/evidence.

Expected implementation direction:
- Add a machine-readable capability contract registry in reigh-worker, likely under scripts/capability_contracts/ or source/task_handlers/tasks/ depending on repo patterns.
- Add a CLI/report generator, e.g. python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report or similar, with commands/status output that can merge: registry, template_routing.py, scripts/live_test/matrix.py, ../vibecomfy/template_index.json, ../vibecomfy/workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json, and latest scripts/live_test/runs/*/report.md.
- Generate or update docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md from data rather than hand-maintaining it.
- Add contract tests that fail if a vibecomfy_supported route lacks a contract row, VibeComfy template reference, output artifact contract, variant accounting, or live evidence pointer.
- Add explicit status vocabulary: unknown/inventoried/routed/template_bound/static_valid/runtime_green/parity_reviewed/app_ready, plus wgp_only_contract_validated and unsupported_fail_closed where appropriate.
- Add next-action/status output so a developer/agent can see what blocks 100% coverage.
- Keep implementation bounded: first slice can be registry + report + tests + generated tracker + using it to identify and close obvious holes. Then continue improving if needed.

Success criteria:
MUST:
- Use megaplan standard workflow with prep -> plan -> critique -> gate -> finalize -> execute -> review.
- Build code, not only docs, unless prep proves there is already an equivalent working tool.
- Do not duplicate VibeComfy workflow validators in reigh-worker; call/reference/consume VibeComfy outputs instead.
- Produce a durable, checked-in capability-contract source of truth and generated/reportable parity status.
- Cover every current live matrix VibeComfy-supported route with a contract row or an explicit justified exception.
- Add tests proving route/template/live-evidence coverage cannot silently drift.
- Keep dirty unrelated app work intact.
SHOULD:
- Include app capability source references where practical.
- Include variant axes for LoRA/control/source media/resolution/frame/FPS/profile/postprocess/output, even if some entries are initially gap/fail-closed.
- Make the developer/agent UX discoverable through commands/docs.
- Use the new tooling after building it to update the tracker and identify next parity gaps.
INFO:
- Future directions include import/fork/new workflow workbench UX, but the first implementation should land the central capability contract and report loop.

canonical_route_key == 'image-upscale':\n        evidence_cases = sorted(set(evidence_cases + case_by_route.get('image_upscale', [])))\n    contract = {\n        'capability_id': capability_id,\n      'blockers': blockers or [],\n    }\n    if implementation == 'vibecomfy' and template_id not in template_ids:\n        contract['blockers'].append(f'VibeComfy template id {template_id} was not present in ../vibecomfy/template_index.json during seed scan')\n    contracts.append(contract)\n    claimed_routes.add(canonical_route_key)\n    claimed_routes.update(route_keys)\n\nfor route, (name,   if entry.support_state != 'vibecomfy_supported' or entry.route_key in claimed_routes:\n        continue\n    task_type = entry.route_key.split('__', 1)[0]\n    if task_type in {'travel_segment', orchestrator'),\n    ('join_clips_orchestrator', ['app.create_task_edge_client', 'app.join_clips'], orchestration_artifact, wgp_axes, 'Join Clips orchestrator'),\n    ('edit_video_orchestrator', add_contract(\n        capability_id='cap.' + route_key,\n        name=name,\n        implementation='wgp',\n        canonical_route_key=route_key,\n        task_types=[route_key],\n        artifact=artifact,\n        axes=axes,\n        app_refs=app_refs,\n        notes=['WGP-only/non-template route; parity validation is DB state, route metadata, and artifact behavior'],\n    )\n\nfor         Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker

        Idea:
Build and use a robust capability-contract and workflow-workbench toolchain that gets Reigh to 100% app parity coverage for VibeComfy vs existing Wan2GP/app behavior, while leaving reusable tools for future internal/community workflows, workflow forks, and scratch-built workflows.

Current situation and context:
- Repos live under /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace.
- Main implementation repo for this run is reigh-worker. Current durable branch should be megaplan/vibecomfy-sprint-09-control-rail-travel-matrix.
- VibeComfy repo is ../vibecomfy on branch megaplan/production-parity-templates.
- reigh-app has a dirty working tree on its own branch; inspect it as source of app capability truth but do not casually commit or revert its changes.
- The user wants actual execution after planning, not just a design doc. Keep going until the new tooling is built, then use it to continue closing validation/parity gaps.
- The user explicitly requested this run in a GPT-5.5 medium subagent. This current agent is that subagent. The local megaplan CLI supports profiles and phase model overrides, but no installed profile exposes an explicit GPT-5.5 medium model string. Use the all-codex profile / Codex-compatible routing where possible and note that limitation.

Important current facts:
- reigh-worker has a new tracker doc at docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md.
- reigh-worker has route contracts in source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py.
- reigh-worker has live matrix in scripts/live_test/matrix.py and live reports in scripts/live_test/runs/*/report.md.
- reigh-worker has VibeComfy adapter/scratchpads in source/models/comfy/vibecomfy_adapter.py.
- reigh-worker has worker preflight checks for VibeComfy template_index.json and workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json in source/runtime/worker/preflight.py.
- reigh-worker has health route labels in source/runtime/worker/health_labels.py.
- VibeComfy already owns workflow/template mechanics. Do not rebuild these in reigh-worker: python -m vibecomfy.cli port check, port convert, port widgets, validate, doctor, nodes install-plan/install/restore, fetch, workflows list, inspect, run, runpod; template_index.json; workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json; ready_templates; model registry/staging; custom-node pack registry; RunPod/corpus machinery.
- A subagent history audit found oldest local VibeComfy commit around 2026-04-26, not March. It concluded: VibeComfy should own Comfy implementation truth; reigh-worker should own app/product task truth, routing, Wan2GP parity, DB/storage artifact semantics, and live app validation.

Validated live-green VibeComfy-supported cases from current tracker include:
- individual_travel_segment_wan22_vace raw/flow/canny/depth
- travel_segment_wan22_vace raw/flow/canny/depth video_source
- join_clips_segment_wan22_vace
- qwen_image_style
- qwen_image_t2i
- qwen_image_2512
- wan_2_2_t2i
- wan_2_2_i2v
- animate_character
- image_upscale
- video_enhance
- flux_klein_edit
- qwen_image_edit
- image_inpaint
- annotated_image_edit
- z_image_turbo
- z_image_turbo_i2i
But these are live-green cases, not proof of full app parity for all variants.

Known gaps to handle in plan/execution:
- Need a central capability id/name per product behavior. Current concepts are messy: task_type, route_key, template_id, workflow id, live case name, and actual product capability are mixed.
- Need capability contract registry/report generator tying app capabilities -> task types -> route keys -> Wan2GP behavior/defaults -> VibeComfy implementation/template/scratchpad -> variant axes -> output artifact contract -> static/live validation evidence.
- Need variant-aware coverage: LoRAs, multiple LoRAs, URL LoRAs, phase-scheduled LoRAs, control methods raw/flow/canny/depth/pose/mask, source media variants, resolution/frame/FPS/profile/seed/prompt/negative prompt/interpolation/postprocess/output.
- Need not brute-force all combinations; use risk-based pairwise/interaction coverage. Every app-exposed axis value needs at least one proof or fail-closed rule; known interactions need explicit cases.
- Need distinguish non-Comfy paths like orchestration/stitching: these may remain WGP/non-template but still need contract validation for child creation, route metadata, DB state, and artifact behavior.
- Need use VibeComfy validators rather than reimplementing graph/schema/custom node/model validation in worker.
- Need tooling that helps agents/developers import, fork, or build workflows from scratch: draft contracts, inspect/explain, validate, diff, next-step guidance, status labels.

Prep phase instructions:
1. Inventory current tooling in reigh-worker, VibeComfy, and relevant reigh-app/Supabase enqueue surfaces.
2. Determine where app capabilities are defined/enqueued, without changing reigh-app unless explicitly necessary.
3. Inspect existing live matrix/report parsing affordances and any docs/tests around migration-baselines, worker matrix, template routing, artifact contracts, VibeComfy preflight.
4. Inspect VibeComfy CLI/docs/tests enough to avoid duplicating its responsibilities.
5. Identify the smallest executable first slice that creates real leverage, not just prose.

Desired high-level architecture:
- Capability = product behavior users care about.
- Route = execution decision for a capability variant.
- Implementation = WGP or VibeComfy binding.
- Template = VibeComfy implementation detail.
- Workflow = source/import artifact.
- Live case = validation sample/evidence.

Expected implementation direction:
- Add a machine-readable capability contract registry in reigh-worker, likely under scripts/capability_contracts/ or source/task_handlers/tasks/ depending on repo patterns.
- Add a CLI/report generator, e.g. python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report or similar, with commands/status output that can merge: registry, template_routing.py, scripts/live_test/matrix.py, ../vibecomfy/template_index.json, ../vibecomfy/workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json, and latest scripts/live_test/runs/*/report.md.
- Generate or update docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md from data rather than hand-maintaining it.
- Add contract tests that fail if a vibecomfy_supported route lacks a contract row, VibeComfy template reference, output artifact contract, variant accounting, or live evidence pointer.
- Add explicit status vocabulary: unknown/inventoried/routed/template_bound/static_valid/runtime_green/parity_reviewed/app_ready, plus wgp_only_contract_validated and unsupported_fail_closed where appropriate.
- Add next-action/status output so a developer/agent can see what blocks 100% coverage.
- Keep implementation bounded: first slice can be registry + report + tests + generated tracker + using it to identify and close obvious holes. Then continue improving if needed.

Success criteria:
MUST:
- Use megaplan standard workflow with prep -> plan -> critique -> gate -> finalize -> execute -> review.
- Build code, not only docs, unless prep proves there is already an equivalent working tool.
- Do not duplicate VibeComfy workflow validators in reigh-worker; call/reference/consume VibeComfy outputs instead.
- Produce a durable, checked-in capability-contract source of truth and generated/reportable parity status.
- Cover every current live matrix VibeComfy-supported route with a contract row or an explicit justified exception.
- Add tests proving route/template/live-evidence coverage cannot silently drift.
- Keep dirty unrelated app work intact.
SHOULD:
- Include app capability source references where practical.
- Include variant axes for LoRA/control/source media/resolution/frame/FPS/profile/postprocess/output, even if some entries are initially gap/fail-closed.
- Make the developer/agent UX discoverable through commands/docs.
- Use the new tooling after building it to update the tracker and identify next parity gaps.
INFO:
- Future directions include import/fork/new workflow workbench UX, but the first implementation should land the central capability contract and report loop.

        Idea:
Build and use a robust capability-contract and workflow-workbench toolchain that gets Reigh to 100% app parity coverage for VibeComfy vs existing Wan2GP/app behavior, while leaving reusable tools for future internal/community workflows, workflow forks, and scratch-built workflows.

Current situation and context:
- Repos live under /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace.
- Main implementation repo for this run is reigh-worker. Current durable branch should be megaplan/vibecomfy-sprint-09-control-rail-travel-matrix.
- VibeComfy repo is ../vibecomfy on branch megaplan/production-parity-templates.
- reigh-app has a dirty working tree on its own branch; inspect it as source of app capability truth but do not casually commit or revert its changes.
- The user wants actual execution after planning, not just a design doc. Keep going until the new tooling is built, then use it to continue closing validation/parity gaps.
- The user explicitly requested this run in a GPT-5.5 medium subagent. This current agent is that subagent. The local megaplan CLI supports profiles and phase model overrides, but no installed profile exposes an explicit GPT-5.5 medium model string. Use the all-codex profile / Codex-compatible routing where possible and note that limitation.

Important current facts:
- reigh-worker has a new tracker doc at docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md.
- reigh-worker has route contracts in source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py.
- reigh-worker has live matrix in scripts/live_test/matrix.py and live reports in scripts/live_test/runs/*/report.md.
- reigh-worker has VibeComfy adapter/scratchpads in source/models/comfy/vibecomfy_adapter.py.
- reigh-worker has worker preflight checks for VibeComfy template_index.json and workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json in source/runtime/worker/preflight.py.
- reigh-worker has health route labels in source/runtime/worker/health_labels.py.
- VibeComfy already owns workflow/template mechanics. Do not rebuild these in reigh-worker: python -m vibecomfy.cli port check, port convert, port widgets, validate, doctor, nodes install-plan/install/restore, fetch, workflows list, inspect, run, runpod; template_index.json; workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json; ready_templates; model registry/staging; custom-node pack registry; RunPod/corpus machinery.
- A subagent history audit found oldest local VibeComfy commit around 2026-04-26, not March. It concluded: VibeComfy should own Comfy implementation truth; reigh-worker should own app/product task truth, routing, Wan2GP parity, DB/storage artifact semantics, and live app validation.

Validated live-green VibeComfy-supported cases from current tracker include:
- individual_travel_segment_wan22_vace raw/flow/canny/depth
- travel_segment_wan22_vace raw/flow/canny/depth video_source
- join_clips_segment_wan22_vace
- qwen_image_style
- qwen_image_t2i
- qwen_image_2512
- wan_2_2_t2i
- wan_2_2_i2v
- animate_character
- image_upscale
- video_enhance
- flux_klein_edit
- qwen_image_edit
- image_inpaint
- annotated_image_edit
- z_image_turbo
- z_image_turbo_i2i
But these are live-green cases, not proof of full app parity for all variants.

Known gaps to handle in plan/execution:
- Need a central capability id/name per product behavior. Current concepts are messy: task_type, route_key, template_id, workflow id, live case name, and actual product capability are mixed.
- Need capability contract registry/report generator tying app capabilities -> task types -> route keys -> Wan2GP behavior/defaults -> VibeComfy implementation/template/scratchpad -> variant axes -> output artifact contract -> static/live validation evidence.
- Need variant-aware coverage: LoRAs, multiple LoRAs, URL LoRAs, phase-scheduled LoRAs, control methods raw/flow/canny/depth/pose/mask, source media variants, resolution/frame/FPS/profile/seed/prompt/negative prompt/interpolation/postprocess/output.
- Need not brute-force all combinations; use risk-based pairwise/interaction coverage. Every app-exposed axis value needs at least one proof or fail-closed rule; known interactions need explicit cases.
- Need distinguish non-Comfy paths like orchestration/stitching: these may remain WGP/non-template but still need contract validation for child creation, route metadata, DB state, and artifact behavior.
- Need use VibeComfy validators rather than reimplementing graph/schema/custom node/model validation in worker.
- Need tooling that helps agents/developers import, fork, or build workflows from scratch: draft contracts, inspect/explain, validate, diff, next-step guidance, status labels.

Prep phase instructions:
1. Inventory current tooling in reigh-worker, VibeComfy, and relevant reigh-app/Supabase enqueue surfaces.
2. Determine where app capabilities are defined/enqueued, without changing reigh-app unless explicitly necessary.
3. Inspect existing live matrix/report parsing affordances and any docs/tests around migration-baselines, worker matrix, template routing, artifact contracts, VibeComfy preflight.
4. Inspect VibeComfy CLI/docs/tests enough to avoid duplicating its responsibilities.
5. Identify the smallest executable first slice that creates real leverage, not just prose.

Desired high-level architecture:
- Capability = product behavior users care about.
- Route = execution decision for a capability variant.
- Implementation = WGP or VibeComfy binding.
- Template = VibeComfy implementation detail.
- Workflow = source/import artifact.
- Live case = validation sample/evidence.

Expected implementation direction:
- Add a machine-readable capability contract registry in reigh-worker, likely under scripts/capability_contracts/ or source/task_handlers/tasks/ depending on repo patterns.
- Add a CLI/report generator, e.g. python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report or similar, with commands/status output that can merge: registry, template_routing.py, scripts/live_test/matrix.py, ../vibecomfy/template_index.json, ../vibecomfy/workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json, and latest scripts/live_test/runs/*/report.md.
- Generate or update docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md from data rather than hand-maintaining it.
- Add contract tests that fail if a vibecomfy_supported route lacks a contract row, VibeComfy template reference, output artifact contract, variant accounting, or live evidence pointer.
- Add explicit status vocabulary: unknown/inventoried/routed/template_bound/static_valid/runtime_green/parity_reviewed/app_ready, plus wgp_only_contract_validated and unsupported_fail_closed where appropriate.
- Add next-action/status output so a developer/agent can see what blocks 100% coverage.
- Keep implementation bounded: first slice can be registry + report + tests + generated tracker + using it to identify and close obvious holes. Then continue improving if needed.

Success criteria:
MUST:
- Use megaplan standard workflow with prep -> plan -> critique -> gate -> finalize -> execute -> review.
- Build code, not only docs, unless prep proves there is already an equivalent working tool.
- Do not duplicate VibeComfy workflow validators in reigh-worker; call/reference/consume VibeComfy outputs instead.
- Produce a durable, checked-in capability-contract source of truth and generated/reportable parity status.
- Cover every current live matrix VibeComfy-supported route with a contract row or an explicit justified exception.
- Add tests proving route/template/live-evidence coverage cannot silently drift.
- Keep dirty unrelated app work intact.
SHOULD:
- Include app capability source references where practical.
- Include variant axes for LoRA/control/source media/resolution/frame/FPS/profile/postprocess/output, even if some entries are initially gap/fail-closed.
- Make the developer/agent UX discoverable through commands/docs.
- Use the new tooling after building it to update the tracker and identify next parity gaps.
INFO:
- Future directions include import/fork/new workflow workbench UX, but the first implementation should land the central capability contract and report loop.

        Idea:
Build and use a robust capability-contract and workflow-workbench toolchain that gets Reigh to 100% app parity coverage for VibeComfy vs existing Wan2GP/app behavior, while leaving reusable tools for future internal/community workflows, workflow forks, and scratch-built workflows.

Current situation and context:
- Repos live under /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace.
- Main implementation repo for this run is reigh-worker. Current durable branch should be megaplan/vibecomfy-sprint-09-control-rail-travel-matrix.
- VibeComfy repo is ../vibecomfy on branch megaplan/production-parity-templates.
- reigh-app has a dirty working tree on its own branch; inspect it as source of app capability truth but do not casually commit or revert its changes.
- The user wants actual execution after planning, not just a design doc. Keep going until the new tooling is built, then use it to continue closing validation/parity gaps.
- The user explicitly requested this run in a GPT-5.5 medium subagent. This current agent is that subagent. The local megaplan CLI supports profiles and phase model overrides, but no installed profile exposes an explicit GPT-5.5 medium model string. Use the all-codex profile / Codex-compatible routing where possible and note that limitation.

Important current facts:
- reigh-worker has a new tracker doc at docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md.
- reigh-worker has route contracts in source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py.
- reigh-worker has live matrix in scripts/live_test/matrix.py and live reports in scripts/live_test/runs/*/report.md.
- reigh-worker has VibeComfy adapter/scratchpads in source/models/comfy/vibecomfy_adapter.py.
- reigh-worker has worker preflight checks for VibeComfy template_index.json and workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json in source/runtime/worker/preflight.py.
- reigh-worker has health route labels in source/runtime/worker/health_labels.py.
- VibeComfy already owns workflow/template mechanics. Do not rebuild these in reigh-worker: python -m vibecomfy.cli port check, port convert, port widgets, validate, doctor, nodes install-plan/install/restore, fetch, workflows list, inspect, run, runpod; template_index.json; workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json; ready_templates; model registry/staging; custom-node pack registry; RunPod/corpus machinery.
- A subagent history audit found oldest local VibeComfy commit around 2026-04-26, not March. It concluded: VibeComfy should own Comfy implementation truth; reigh-worker should own app/product task truth, routing, Wan2GP parity, DB/storage artifact semantics, and live app validation.

Validated live-green VibeComfy-supported cases from current tracker include:
- individual_travel_segment_wan22_vace raw/flow/canny/depth
- travel_segment_wan22_vace raw/flow/canny/depth video_source
- join_clips_segment_wan22_vace
- qwen_image_style
- qwen_image_t2i
- qwen_image_2512
- wan_2_2_t2i
- wan_2_2_i2v
- animate_character
- image_upscale
- video_enhance
- flux_klein_edit
- qwen_image_edit
- image_inpaint
- annotated_image_edit
- z_image_turbo
- z_image_turbo_i2i
But these are live-green cases, not proof of full app parity for all variants.

Known gaps to handle in plan/execution:
- Need a central capability id/name per product behavior. Current concepts are messy: task_type, route_key, template_id, workflow id, live case name, and actual product capability are mixed.
- Need capability contract registry/report generator tying app capabilities -> task types -> route keys -> Wan2GP behavior/defaults -> VibeComfy implementation/template/scratchpad -> variant axes -> output artifact contract -> static/live validation evidence.
- Need variant-aware coverage: LoRAs, multiple LoRAs, URL LoRAs, phase-scheduled LoRAs, control methods raw/flow/canny/depth/pose/mask, source media variants, resolution/frame/FPS/profile/seed/prompt/negative prompt/interpolation/postprocess/output.
- Need not brute-force all combinations; use risk-based pairwise/interaction coverage. Every app-exposed axis value needs at least one proof or fail-closed rule; known interactions need explicit cases.
- Need distinguish non-Comfy paths like orchestration/stitching: these may remain WGP/non-template but still need contract validation for child creation, route metadata, DB state, and artifact behavior.
- Need use VibeComfy validators rather than reimplementing graph/schema/custom node/model validation in worker.
- Need tooling that helps agents/developers import, fork, or build workflows from scratch: draft contracts, inspect/explain, validate, diff, next-step guidance, status labels.

Prep phase instructions:
1. Inventory current tooling in reigh-worker, VibeComfy, and relevant reigh-app/Supabase enqueue surfaces.
2. Determine where app capabilities are defined/enqueued, without changing reigh-app unless explicitly necessary.
3. Inspect existing live matrix/report parsing affordances and any docs/tests around migration-baselines, worker matrix, template routing, artifact contracts, VibeComfy preflight.
4. Inspect VibeComfy CLI/docs/tests enough to avoid duplicating its responsibilities.
5. Identify the smallest executable first slice that creates real leverage, not just prose.

Desired high-level architecture:
- Capability = product behavior users care about.
- Route = execution decision for a capability variant.
- Implementation = WGP or VibeComfy binding.
- Template = VibeComfy implementation detail.
- Workflow = source/import artifact.
- Live case = validation sample/evidence.

Expected implementation direction:
- Add a machine-readable capability contract registry in reigh-worker, likely under scripts/capability_contracts/ or source/task_handlers/tasks/ depending on repo patterns.
- Add a CLI/report generator, e.g. python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report or similar, with commands/status output that can merge: registry, template_routing.py, scripts/live_test/matrix.py, ../vibecomfy/template_index.json, ../vibecomfy/workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json, and latest scripts/live_test/runs/*/report.md.
- Generate or update docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md from data rather than hand-maintaining it.
- Add contract tests that fail if a vibecomfy_supported route lacks a contract row, VibeComfy template reference, output artifact contract, variant accounting, or live evidence pointer.
- Add explicit status vocabulary: unknown/inventoried/routed/template_bound/static_valid/runtime_green/parity_reviewed/app_ready, plus wgp_only_contract_validated and unsupported_fail_closed where appropriate.
- Add next-action/status output so a developer/agent can see what blocks 100% coverage.
- Keep implementation bounded: first slice can be registry + report + tests + generated tracker + using it to identify and close obvious holes. Then continue improving if needed.

Success criteria:
MUST:
- Use megaplan standard workflow with prep -> plan -> critique -> gate -> finalize -> execute -> review.
- Build code, not only docs, unless prep proves there is already an equivalent working tool.
- Do not duplicate VibeComfy workflow validators in reigh-worker; call/reference/consume VibeComfy outputs instead.
- Produce a durable, checked-in capability-contract source of truth and generated/reportable parity status.
- Cover every current live matrix VibeComfy-supported route with a contract row or an explicit justified exception.
- Add tests proving route/template/live-evidence coverage cannot silently drift.
- Keep dirty unrelated app work intact.
SHOULD:
- Include app capability source references where practical.
- Include variant axes for LoRA/control/source media/resolution/frame/FPS/profile/postprocess/output, even if some entries are initially gap/fail-closed.
- Make the developer/agent UX discoverable through commands/docs.
- Use the new tooling after building it to update the tracker and identify next parity gaps.
INFO:
- Future directions include import/fork/new workflow workbench UX, but the first implementation should land the central capability contract and report loop.

body:\n+        body = [\"| \" + \" | \".join(\"\" for _ in headers) + \" |\"]\n+    return \"\\n\".join([header_line, separator, *body])\n+\n+\n+def _escape_table_cell(value: str) -> str:\n+    return value.replace(\"\\n\", \"<br>\").replace(\"|\", \"\\\\|\")\n+\n+\n+def _yes_no(value: bool) -> str:\n+    return \"yes\" if value else \"no\"\n+\n+\n+def _app_scan_status(capability_id: str, app_inventory: Any) -> str:\n+    if capability_id in app_inventory.missing_sources:\n+        return \"missing source\"\n+    if capability_id in app_inventory.missing_literals:\n+        return \"missing literals: \" + \", \".join(app_inventory.missing_literals[capability_id])\n+    return \"ok\"\n+\n+\n+def _join_code(values: Sequence[str]) -> str:\n+    return \", \".join(f\"`{value}`\" for value in values)\n+\n+\n+def _join_text(values: Sequence[str]) -> str:\n+    return \"<br>\".join(values)\n+\n+\n+def _blocker_rows(\n+    contracts: Sequence[Any],\n+    validation: dict[str, Any],\n+) -> list[tuple[str, str, str, str]]:\n+    rows: list[tuple[str, str, str, str]] = []\n+    for contract in contracts:\n+        for blocker in contract.blockers:\n+            rows.append((\"blocker\", \"contract\", contract.capability_id, blocker))\n+    for message in validation[\"messages\"]:\n+        if message[\"level\"] in {\"error\", \"warning\"}:\n+            target = message.get(\"capability_id\") or message.get(\"route_key\") or message.get(\"path\") or \"(global)\"\n+            rows.append((message[\"level\"], message[\"category\"], target, message[\"message\"]))\n+    return sorted(set(rows), key=lambda row: row)\n+\n+\n+def _variant_rows(contracts: Sequence[Any]) -> list[tuple[str, str, str, str, str]]:\n+    rows: list[tuple[str, str, str, str, str]] = []\n+    for contract in contracts:\n+        for axis in contract.variant_axes:\n+            rows.append(\n+                (\n+                    f\"`{contract.capability_id}`\",\n+                    f\"`{axis.name}`\",\n+                    _join_code(axis.values),\n+                    axis.coverage or \"\",\n+                    _yes_no(axis.fail_closed),\n+                )\n+            )\n+    return rows\n*** End Patch\n"
}

This package consumes VibeComfy\n+manifests and points developers to VibeComfy commands; it does not reimplement\n+graph, schema, custom-node, or model checks in `reigh-worker`.\n+\n+## Files\n+\n+- `contracts.json`: capability contracts keyed by product behavior.\n+- `app_capabilities.json`: read-only app source inventory and literals that\n+  should exist in `../reigh-app`.\n+- `live_matrix_manifest.json`: stable metadata for live matrix cases without\n+  importing `scripts.live_test.matrix`.\n+- `report.py`: deterministic CLI for status, validation, rendering, and next\n+  actions.\n+\n+## Commands\n+\n+```bash\n+python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report status --json\n+python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report app-inventory --json\n+python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report next-actions\n+python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report validate\n+python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report render docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md\n+python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report render --check docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md\n+```\n+\n+Use `VIBECOMFY_PATH=/path/to/vibecomfy` when the VibeComfy checkout is not at\n+`../vibecomfy`.\n+\n+## Add Or Change A Capability\n+\n+1. Add or update an app inventory row in `app_capabilities.json` with the\n+   relevant read-only `../reigh-app` source file and expected literals.\n+2. Add or update a row in `contracts.json` with:\n+   - stable `capability_id` and product-facing `name`\n+   - `canonical_route_key` and any `route_keys` aliases\n+   - implementation: `vibecomfy`, `wgp`, or `unsupported`\n+   - VibeComfy `template_id` only when implementation is `vibecomfy`\n+   - explicit `variant_axes`, including covered values and fail-closed rules\n+   - `artifact_contract` for DB state, storage paths, and output kinds\n+   - `app_refs`, `live_evidence`, `static_evidence`, notes, and blockers\n+3. Add or update `live_matrix_manifest.json` only for stable live evidence\n+   metadata. Do not import live-test modules from report-time code.\n+4. Run `python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report validate`.\n+5. Run `python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report next-actions` and close\n+   worker-local omissions before treating remaining items as app or VibeComfy\n+   blockers.\n+6. Regenerate the tracker with `render`.\n+\n+## Validation Boundary\n+\n+`reigh-worker` owns product/app parity accounting, route coverage, Wan2GP\n+behavior references, DB/storage artifact semantics, and live evidence links.\n+\n+VibeComfy owns workflow/template internals. For template graph/schema/custom\n+node/model checks, use VibeComfy-owned commands from the VibeComfy checkout:\n+\n+```bash\n+cd ../vibecomfy\n+python -m vibecomfy.cli validate\n+python -m vibecomfy.cli doctor\n+python -m vibecomfy.cli workflows list\n+python -m vibecomfy.cli workflows inspect <workflow-id>\n+```\n+\n+If a worker report points at missing VibeComfy template-index evidence, record a\n+blocker in `contracts.json` or update VibeComfy artifacts in that repo. Do not\n+patch around VibeComfy validation inside `reigh-worker`.\n+\n+## App Inventory Expectations\n+\n+The app inventory is intentionally lightweight. It should prove that a\n+capability row still points at real app enqueue/configuration truth without\n+requiring this repo to import, build, or mutate `../reigh-app`.\n+\n+Each inventory row should name a source file and literals or resolver ids that\n+would disappear if app enqueue behavior drifted. Missing files or literals are\n+validation errors.\n+\n+## Workflow Workbench Direction\n+\n+Future import, fork, and scratch-built workflow tooling should build on these\n+contracts:\n+\n+- draft a capability contract before a workflow is ported\n+- link imported workflow ids and VibeComfy template ids\n+- preserve route aliases rather than duplicating product capabilities\n+- show next actions by app inventory, route binding, static evidence, live\n+  evidence, artifacts, variants, aliases, and parity review\n+- keep reusable workflow mechanics in VibeComfy and product/app parity evidence\n+  in `reigh-worker`\n*** End Patch\n"

        Idea:
Build and use a robust capability-contract and workflow-workbench toolchain that gets Reigh to 100% app parity coverage for VibeComfy vs existing Wan2GP/app behavior, while leaving reusable tools for future internal/community workflows, workflow forks, and scratch-built workflows.

Current situation and context:
- Repos live under /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace.
- Main implementation repo for this run is reigh-worker. Current durable branch should be megaplan/vibecomfy-sprint-09-control-rail-travel-matrix.
- VibeComfy repo is ../vibecomfy on branch megaplan/production-parity-templates.
- reigh-app has a dirty working tree on its own branch; inspect it as source of app capability truth but do not casually commit or revert its changes.
- The user wants actual execution after planning, not just a design doc. Keep going until the new tooling is built, then use it to continue closing validation/parity gaps.
- The user explicitly requested this run in a GPT-5.5 medium subagent. This current agent is that subagent. The local megaplan CLI supports profiles and phase model overrides, but no installed profile exposes an explicit GPT-5.5 medium model string. Use the all-codex profile / Codex-compatible routing where possible and note that limitation.

Important current facts:
- reigh-worker has a new tracker doc at docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md.
- reigh-worker has route contracts in source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py.
- reigh-worker has live matrix in scripts/live_test/matrix.py and live reports in scripts/live_test/runs/*/report.md.
- reigh-worker has VibeComfy adapter/scratchpads in source/models/comfy/vibecomfy_adapter.py.
- reigh-worker has worker preflight checks for VibeComfy template_index.json and workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json in source/runtime/worker/preflight.py.
- reigh-worker has health route labels in source/runtime/worker/health_labels.py.
- VibeComfy already owns workflow/template mechanics. Do not rebuild these in reigh-worker: python -m vibecomfy.cli port check, port convert, port widgets, validate, doctor, nodes install-plan/install/restore, fetch, workflows list, inspect, run, runpod; template_index.json; workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json; ready_templates; model registry/staging; custom-node pack registry; RunPod/corpus machinery.
- A subagent history audit found oldest local VibeComfy commit around 2026-04-26, not March. It concluded: VibeComfy should own Comfy implementation truth; reigh-worker should own app/product task truth, routing, Wan2GP parity, DB/storage artifact semantics, and live app validation.

Validated live-green VibeComfy-supported cases from current tracker include:
- individual_travel_segment_wan22_vace raw/flow/canny/depth
- travel_segment_wan22_vace raw/flow/canny/depth video_source
- join_clips_segment_wan22_vace
- qwen_image_style
- qwen_image_t2i
- qwen_image_2512
- wan_2_2_t2i
- wan_2_2_i2v
- animate_character
- image_upscale
- video_enhance
- flux_klein_edit
- qwen_image_edit
- image_inpaint
- annotated_image_edit
- z_image_turbo
- z_image_turbo_i2i
But these are live-green cases, not proof of full app parity for all variants.

Known gaps to handle in plan/execution:
- Need a central capability id/name per product behavior. Current concepts are messy: task_type, route_key, template_id, workflow id, live case name, and actual product capability are mixed.
- Need capability contract registry/report generator tying app capabilities -> task types -> route keys -> Wan2GP behavior/defaults -> VibeComfy implementation/template/scratchpad -> variant axes -> output artifact contract -> static/live validation evidence.
- Need variant-aware coverage: LoRAs, multiple LoRAs, URL LoRAs, phase-scheduled LoRAs, control methods raw/flow/canny/depth/pose/mask, source media variants, resolution/frame/FPS/profile/seed/prompt/negative prompt/interpolation/postprocess/output.
- Need not brute-force all combinations; use risk-based pairwise/interaction coverage. Every app-exposed axis value needs at least one proof or fail-closed rule; known interactions need explicit cases.
- Need distinguish non-Comfy paths like orchestration/stitching: these may remain WGP/non-template but still need contract validation for child creation, route metadata, DB state, and artifact behavior.
- Need use VibeComfy validators rather than reimplementing graph/schema/custom node/model validation in worker.
- Need tooling that helps agents/developers import, fork, or build workflows from scratch: draft contracts, inspect/explain, validate, diff, next-step guidance, status labels.

Prep phase instructions:
1. Inventory current tooling in reigh-worker, VibeComfy, and relevant reigh-app/Supabase enqueue surfaces.
2. Determine where app capabilities are defined/enqueued, without changing reigh-app unless explicitly necessary.
3. Inspect existing live matrix/report parsing affordances and any docs/tests around migration-baselines, worker matrix, template routing, artifact contracts, VibeComfy preflight.
4. Inspect VibeComfy CLI/docs/tests enough to avoid duplicating its responsibilities.
5. Identify the smallest executable first slice that creates real leverage, not just prose.

Desired high-level architecture:
- Capability = product behavior users care about.
- Route = execution decision for a capability variant.
- Implementation = WGP or VibeComfy binding.
- Template = VibeComfy implementation detail.
- Workflow = source/import artifact.
- Live case = validation sample/evidence.

Expected implementation direction:
- Add a machine-readable capability contract registry in reigh-worker, likely under scripts/capability_contracts/ or source/task_handlers/tasks/ depending on repo patterns.
- Add a CLI/report generator, e.g. python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report or similar, with commands/status output that can merge: registry, template_routing.py, scripts/live_test/matrix.py, ../vibecomfy/template_index.json, ../vibecomfy/workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json, and latest scripts/live_test/runs/*/report.md.
- Generate or update docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md from data rather than hand-maintaining it.
- Add contract tests that fail if a vibecomfy_supported route lacks a contract row, VibeComfy template reference, output artifact contract, variant accounting, or live evidence pointer.
- Add explicit status vocabulary: unknown/inventoried/routed/template_bound/static_valid/runtime_green/parity_reviewed/app_ready, plus wgp_only_contract_validated and unsupported_fail_closed where appropriate.
- Add next-action/status output so a developer/agent can see what blocks 100% coverage.
- Keep implementation bounded: first slice can be registry + report + tests + generated tracker + using it to identify and close obvious holes. Then continue improving if needed.

Success criteria:
MUST:
- Use megaplan standard workflow with prep -> plan -> critique -> gate -> finalize -> execute -> review.
- Build code, not only docs, unless prep proves there is already an equivalent working tool.
- Do not duplicate VibeComfy workflow validators in reigh-worker; call/reference/consume VibeComfy outputs instead.
- Produce a durable, checked-in capability-contract source of truth and generated/reportable parity status.
- Cover every current live matrix VibeComfy-supported route with a contract row or an explicit justified exception.
- Add tests proving route/template/live-evidence coverage cannot silently drift.
- Keep dirty unrelated app work intact.
SHOULD:
- Include app capability source references where practical.
- Include variant axes for LoRA/control/source media/resolution/frame/FPS/profile/postprocess/output, even if some entries are initially gap/fail-closed.
- Make the developer/agent UX discoverable through commands/docs.
- Use the new tooling after building it to update the tracker and identify next parity gaps.
INFO:
- Future directions include import/fork/new workflow workbench UX, but the first implementation should land the central capability contract and report loop.

        Idea:
Build and use a robust capability-contract and workflow-workbench toolchain that gets Reigh to 100% app parity coverage for VibeComfy vs existing Wan2GP/app behavior, while leaving reusable tools for future internal/community workflows, workflow forks, and scratch-built workflows.

Current situation and context:
- Repos live under /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace.
- Main implementation repo for this run is reigh-worker. Current durable branch should be megaplan/vibecomfy-sprint-09-control-rail-travel-matrix.
- VibeComfy repo is ../vibecomfy on branch megaplan/production-parity-templates.
- reigh-app has a dirty working tree on its own branch; inspect it as source of app capability truth but do not casually commit or revert its changes.
- The user wants actual execution after planning, not just a design doc. Keep going until the new tooling is built, then use it to continue closing validation/parity gaps.
- The user explicitly requested this run in a GPT-5.5 medium subagent. This current agent is that subagent. The local megaplan CLI supports profiles and phase model overrides, but no installed profile exposes an explicit GPT-5.5 medium model string. Use the all-codex profile / Codex-compatible routing where possible and note that limitation.

Important current facts:
- reigh-worker has a new tracker doc at docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md.
- reigh-worker has route contracts in source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py.
- reigh-worker has live matrix in scripts/live_test/matrix.py and live reports in scripts/live_test/runs/*/report.md.
- reigh-worker has VibeComfy adapter/scratchpads in source/models/comfy/vibecomfy_adapter.py.
- reigh-worker has worker preflight checks for VibeComfy template_index.json and workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json in source/runtime/worker/preflight.py.
- reigh-worker has health route labels in source/runtime/worker/health_labels.py.
- VibeComfy already owns workflow/template mechanics. Do not rebuild these in reigh-worker: python -m vibecomfy.cli port check, port convert, port widgets, validate, doctor, nodes install-plan/install/restore, fetch, workflows list, inspect, run, runpod; template_index.json; workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json; ready_templates; model registry/staging; custom-node pack registry; RunPod/corpus machinery.
- A subagent history audit found oldest local VibeComfy commit around 2026-04-26, not March. It concluded: VibeComfy should own Comfy implementation truth; reigh-worker should own app/product task truth, routing, Wan2GP parity, DB/storage artifact semantics, and live app validation.

Validated live-green VibeComfy-supported cases from current tracker include:
- individual_travel_segment_wan22_vace raw/flow/canny/depth
- travel_segment_wan22_vace raw/flow/canny/depth video_source
- join_clips_segment_wan22_vace
- qwen_image_style
- qwen_image_t2i
- qwen_image_2512
- wan_2_2_t2i
- wan_2_2_i2v
- animate_character
- image_upscale
- video_enhance
- flux_klein_edit
- qwen_image_edit
- image_inpaint
- annotated_image_edit
- z_image_turbo
- z_image_turbo_i2i
But these are live-green cases, not proof of full app parity for all variants.

Known gaps to handle in plan/execution:
- Need a central capability id/name per product behavior. Current concepts are messy: task_type, route_key, template_id, workflow id, live case name, and actual product capability are mixed.
- Need capability contract registry/report generator tying app capabilities -> task types -> route keys -> Wan2GP behavior/defaults -> VibeComfy implementation/template/scratchpad -> variant axes -> output artifact contract -> static/live validation evidence.
- Need variant-aware coverage: LoRAs, multiple LoRAs, URL LoRAs, phase-scheduled LoRAs, control methods raw/flow/canny/depth/pose/mask, source media variants, resolution/frame/FPS/profile/seed/prompt/negative prompt/interpolation/postprocess/output.
- Need not brute-force all combinations; use risk-based pairwise/interaction coverage. Every app-exposed axis value needs at least one proof or fail-closed rule; known interactions need explicit cases.
- Need distinguish non-Comfy paths like orchestration/stitching: these may remain WGP/non-template but still need contract validation for child creation, route metadata, DB state, and artifact behavior.
- Need use VibeComfy validators rather than reimplementing graph/schema/custom node/model validation in worker.
- Need tooling that helps agents/developers import, fork, or build workflows from scratch: draft contracts, inspect/explain, validate, diff, next-step guidance, status labels.

Prep phase instructions:
1. Inventory current tooling in reigh-worker, VibeComfy, and relevant reigh-app/Supabase enqueue surfaces.
2. Determine where app capabilities are defined/enqueued, without changing reigh-app unless explicitly necessary.
3. Inspect existing live matrix/report parsing affordances and any docs/tests around migration-baselines, worker matrix, template routing, artifact contracts, VibeComfy preflight.
4. Inspect VibeComfy CLI/docs/tests enough to avoid duplicating its responsibilities.
5. Identify the smallest executable first slice that creates real leverage, not just prose.

Desired high-level architecture:
- Capability = product behavior users care about.
- Route = execution decision for a capability variant.
- Implementation = WGP or VibeComfy binding.
- Template = VibeComfy implementation detail.
- Workflow = source/import artifact.
- Live case = validation sample/evidence.

Expected implementation direction:
- Add a machine-readable capability contract registry in reigh-worker, likely under scripts/capability_contracts/ or source/task_handlers/tasks/ depending on repo patterns.
- Add a CLI/report generator, e.g. python -m scripts.capability_contracts.report or similar, with commands/status output that can merge: registry, template_routing.py, scripts/live_test/matrix.py, ../vibecomfy/template_index.json, ../vibecomfy/workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json, and latest scripts/live_test/runs/*/report.md.
- Generate or update docs/vibecomfy-app-parity-tracker.md from data rather than hand-maintaining it.
- Add contract tests that fail if a vibecomfy_supported route lacks a contract row, VibeComfy template reference, output artifact contract, variant accounting, or live evidence pointer.
- Add explicit status vocabulary: unknown/inventoried/routed/template_bound/static_valid/runtime_green/parity_reviewed/app_ready, plus wgp_only_contract_validated and unsupported_fail_closed where appropriate.
- Add next-action/status output so a developer/agent can see what blocks 100% coverage.
- Keep implementation bounded: first slice can be registry + report + tests + generated tracker + using it to identify and close obvious holes. Then continue improving if needed.

Success criteria:
MUST:
- Use megaplan standard workflow with prep -> plan -> critique -> gate -> finalize -> execute -> review.
- Build code, not only docs, unless prep proves there is already an equivalent working tool.
- Do not duplicate VibeComfy workflow validators in reigh-worker; call/reference/consume VibeComfy outputs instead.
- Produce a durable, checked-in capability-contract source of truth and generated/reportable parity status.
- Cover every current live matrix VibeComfy-supported route with a contract row or an explicit justified exception.
- Add tests proving route/template/live-evidence coverage cannot silently drift.
- Keep dirty unrelated app work intact.
SHOULD:
- Include app capability source references where practical.
- Include variant axes for LoRA/control/source media/resolution/frame/FPS/profile/postprocess/output, even if some entries are initially gap/fail-closed.
- Make the developer/agent UX discoverable through commands/docs.
- Use the new tooling after building it to update the tracker and identify next parity gaps.
INFO:
- Future directions include import/fork/new workflow workbench UX, but the first implementation should land the central capability contract and report loop.
